# Full model replies, letters revealed one at a time

For a handful of the benchmark clues, the complete visible reply each model gave at every reveal step. No `reasoning_effort` is set, so the word-solver prompt asks the model to reason briefly and then answer, and that reasoning is what you see below. Hidden/internal reasoning (for reasoning models) is not returned by the gateway and is not here.

Models: `gpt-4o-mini`, `gpt-4.1`, `claude-sonnet-4-6`.
